Collaborator
Summary
integrated sparkjs https://sparkjs.dev/, built by world labs to support 3dgs.
Add 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) support using @sparkjsdev/spark library
Add PLY file format support with multiple rendering engines
Support new file formats: .ply, .spz, .splat, .ksplat
Add PLY Engine setting with three options: threejs (mesh), fastply (optimized ASCII point clouds), sparkjs (3DGS)
Add FastPLYLoader for 4-5x faster ASCII PLY parsing
Add original(Advanced) material mode for point cloud rendering with THREE.Points
3dgs generated by https://marble.worldlabs.ai/
test ply file from:
made by https://github.com/PozzettiAndrea/ComfyUI-DepthAnythingV3
threejs offically repo

Contributor
📝 Walkthrough
Walkthrough
Adds PLY/splat support: workspace dependency and PLYEngine setting; new PLY parsers/loaders (FastPLYLoader, ASCII parser, optional Spark SplatMesh); exposes isSplatModel/isPlyModel flags to composables and UI; adds pointCloud material mode; updates loader, scene manager, service, and UI to handle PLY/splat models.
Changes
Cohort / File(s)
Change Summary
Dependencies & Workspace
package.json, pnpm-workspace.yaml
Added @sparkjsdev/spark dependency / catalog entry.
Composable State
src/composables/useLoad3d.ts, src/composables/useLoad3dViewer.ts
Added reactive isSplatModel and isPlyModel flags; populated on load and exposed in return objects.
Components & UI
src/components/load3d/Load3D.vue, src/components/load3d/Load3DControls.vue, src/components/load3d/Load3dViewerContent.vue, src/components/load3d/controls/*/ModelControls.vue, src/components/load3d/controls/viewer/ViewerModelControls.vue
Added props isSplatModel/isPlyModel (and hideMaterialMode binding); hide material/light/export controls for splat models; include pointCloud option for PLY models; pass flags to child controls.
Loader Core & Settings
src/extensions/core/load3d.ts, src/schemas/apiSchema.ts, src/extensions/core/load3d/interfaces.ts
Added Comfy.Load3D.PLYEngine setting (threejs
Loader Manager & Helpers
src/extensions/core/load3d/LoaderManager.ts, src/extensions/core/load3d/loader/FastPLYLoader.ts, src/scripts/metadata/ply.ts
Added FastPLYLoader class; ASCII PLY parser (parseASCIIPLY, isPLYAsciiFormat); LoaderManager additions: plyLoader, fastPlyLoader, currentLoadId, fetchModelData, loadPLY, loadSplat; handles sparkjs path, ASCII detection, vertex color handling, Points vs Mesh creation.
Scene Model Management
src/extensions/core/load3d/SceneModelManager.ts
Added containsSplatMesh() and handlePLYModeSwitch(); special-case setup for SplatMesh; recreate models for material mode switches; preserve MainModel naming and emit materialModeChange.
Load3d API
src/extensions/core/load3d/Load3d.ts
Added public methods isSplatModel() and isPlyModel().
Service Layer
src/services/load3dService.ts
copyLoad3dState() now reloads splat models from originalURL instead of cloning; reorganized state/material/up-direction propagation.
Export Guards
src/extensions/core/saveMesh.ts
Skip export menu items when model is a splat model (early return).
Localization
src/locales/en/main.json
Added load3d.materialModes.pointCloud ("Point Cloud").
Tests & Test Setup
tests-ui/tests/composables/useLoad3d.test.ts, tests-ui/tests/scripts/metadata/ply.test.ts, vitest.setup.ts
Added mocks for isSplatModel/isPlyModel; added tests for ASCII PLY parser; mocked @sparkjsdev/spark SplatMesh in Vitest.
Build Chunking
vite.config.mts
vendor-three chunking now groups ids including @sparkjsdev in addition to three.
Sequence Diagram(s)
```
sequenceDiagram
    participant UI as User/UI
    participant Load3D as Load3D Component
    participant Composable as useLoad3d / useLoad3dViewer
    participant LoaderMgr as LoaderManager
    participant SceneMgr as SceneModelManager
    participant Three as Three.js Scene

    UI->>Load3D: upload/select .ply/.splat/.spz
    Load3D->>Composable: loadModel(path)
    Composable->>LoaderMgr: loadModelInternal(path)

    alt PLYEngine == sparkjs
        LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: construct SplatMesh (spark)
        LoaderMgr->>SceneMgr: return SplatMesh group
    else
        LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: fetch -> arrayBuffer
        LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: isASCII? -> parseASCIIPLY / FastPLY / PLYLoader.parse
        LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: compute normals, detect vertex colors
        alt material mode == pointCloud
            LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: create Points (point cloud)
        else
            LoaderMgr->>LoaderMgr: create Mesh
        end
        LoaderMgr->>SceneMgr: return Object3D
    end

    SceneMgr->>SceneMgr: containsSplatMesh? -> special setup or handlePLYModeSwitch
    SceneMgr->>Three: add model to scene (fit/camera/materials)
    SceneMgr->>Composable: notify model ready
    Composable->>Composable: set isSplatModel / isPlyModel
    Composable->>Load3D: propagate flags
    Load3D->>UI: adjust controls (hide material mode / enable pointCloud)
```

Learnt from: henrikvilhelmberglund
Repo: Comfy-Org/ComfyUI_frontend PR: 7617
File: src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue:301-308
Timestamp: 2025-12-18T16:03:02.066Z
Learning: In the ComfyUI frontend queue system, useQueuePendingTaskCountStore().count indicates the number of tasks in the queue, where count = 1 means a single active/running task and count > 1 means there are pending tasks in addition to the active task. Therefore, in src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue, enable the 'Clear Pending Tasks' button only when count > 1 to avoid clearing the currently running task. The active task should be canceled using the 'Cancel current run' button instead. This rule should be enforced via a conditional check on the queue count, with appropriate disabled/aria-disabled states for accessibility, and tests should verify behavior for count = 1 and count > 1.
```

Consider whether splat models should support these runtime changes or if UI controls should be disabled for splat models to prevent unintended visual behavior.

src/extensions/core/load3d/SceneModelManager.ts (1)
286-288: Type assertion violates coding guidelines—fix the underlying type issue.
The originalMaterials WeakMap is typed for THREE.Mesh but here stores a THREE.Points object using as unknown as THREE.Mesh. This type coercion hides a design issue.
🔎 Consider these solutions:
Option 1: Widen the WeakMap key type to accept both Mesh and Points:
```
-  originalMaterials: WeakMap<THREE.Mesh, THREE.Material | THREE.Material[]> =
-    new WeakMap()
+  originalMaterials: WeakMap<THREE.Mesh | THREE.Points, THREE.Material | THREE.Material[]> =
+    new WeakMap()
```
Then remove the cast:
```
-      this.modelManager.originalMaterials.set(
-        plyPoints as unknown as THREE.Mesh,
-        pointMaterial
-      )
+      this.modelManager.originalMaterials.set(plyPoints, pointMaterial)
```
Option 2: Use a union type alias for clarity:
```
+type RenderableObject = THREE.Mesh | THREE.Points
-  originalMaterials: WeakMap<THREE.Mesh, THREE.Material | THREE.Material[]> =
-    new WeakMap()
+  originalMaterials: WeakMap<RenderableObject, THREE.Material | THREE.Material[]> =
+    new WeakMap()
```
This change would also require updating related traversal code that checks child instanceof THREE.Mesh to also handle THREE.Points.
As per coding guidelines: avoid as type assertions and fix the underlying type issue.

```

Learning: In Vue 3.5+ with <script setup>, when using defineProps<Props>() with partial destructuring (e.g., const { as = 'button', class: customClass = '' } = defineProps<Props>() ), props that are not destructured (e.g., variant, size) stay accessible by name in the template scope. This pattern is valid: you can destructure only a subset of props for convenience while referencing the remaining props directly in template expressions. Apply this guideline to Vue components across the codebase (all .vue files).

♻️ Duplicate comments (10)
src/locales/en/main.json (1)
1638-1638: Address the inconsistent key naming flagged in previous review.
The key "original(Advanced)" was previously flagged as inconsistent with other material mode keys in this section. The naming pattern deviates from the camelCase/lowercase convention used by other keys (normal, wireframe, original).
As previously suggested, consider renaming to:
"pointCloud" or
"originalAdvanced"
Then update all references in the codebase to use the new key name.
src/services/load3dService.ts (1)
78-102: Splat-model copying silently fails without originalURL.
The splat-model branch correctly differentiates splat models from cloneable models. However, when originalURL is missing, the method silently skips model copying entirely, leaving the target in an inconsistent state.
This is a duplicate of a previous review concern. Consider adding explicit error handling or a fallback when originalURL is falsy for splat models.
src/composables/useLoad3d.ts (1)
66-66: isSplatModel state not reset on new model load attempt.
If a splat model is successfully loaded (setting isSplatModel to true) and the next load fails, isSplatModel retains the stale value. The state is set in modelLoadingEnd (line 494) but never cleared in modelLoadingStart.
This is a duplicate of a previous review concern. Consider resetting isSplatModel.value = false in modelLoadingStart to ensure UI consistency during new load attempts.
src/extensions/core/load3d/loader/FastPLYLoader.ts (4)
9-48: Missing validation for PLY file format.
The parser decodes and parses the header without validating:
The PLY magic number at the start of the file
Required vertex properties (x, y, z) are present
Vertex count is non-zero
These omissions can lead to silent failures or corrupted geometry when loading malformed PLY files.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Consider adding validation after line 11 to check the first line starts with "ply", and after header parsing to ensure x/y/z indices are valid and vertexCount > 0.
71-73: parseFloat can silently produce NaN coordinates.
If the PLY file contains non-numeric data or the property indices are incorrect, parseFloat returns NaN without any indication, leading to corrupted geometry.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Consider validating parsed coordinates with Number.isFinite() and either throwing a descriptive error or skipping malformed vertices.
84-86: Missing radix in parseInt calls.
parseInt without an explicit radix can produce unexpected results with certain string formats. Always specify radix 10 for decimal parsing.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Change to parseInt(parts[...], 10) for all three color channel parsings.
50-98: Pre-allocated buffer may contain trailing zeros.
The positions and colors buffers are pre-allocated based on vertexCount, but lines 63 and 66 skip blank lines and lines with insufficient parts. This leaves trailing zeros when fewer vertices are actually parsed.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Consider trimming the buffers after parsing by creating subarrays when vertexIndex < vertexCount before creating the buffer attributes.
src/extensions/core/load3d/SceneModelManager.ts (3)
111-131: Redundant scene traversal for MainModel removal.
The scene is traversed to find and remove MainModel instances, then traversed again at lines 206-212 with a "Double check" comment. This redundancy suggests uncertainty about the cleanup logic and impacts performance.
This duplicates a previous review concern. If the first removal is working correctly, the second should be unnecessary. Consider removing the redundant check or documenting why it's needed.
107-107: Geometry cloning without error handling.
The direct call to this.originalModel.clone() could throw if the PLY geometry is corrupted or malformed, potentially crashing the rendering process.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Consider wrapping the clone operation in a try-catch block or validating the geometry has a position attribute before cloning.
471-480: containsSplatMesh traverses entire scene graph unnecessarily.
The traverse method continues iterating through all children even after finding a SplatMesh, which is inefficient for large models.
This duplicates a previous review concern. Consider using a manual stack-based traversal that returns early when a SplatMesh is found, or use a recursive helper that short-circuits.

Learnt from: henrikvilhelmberglund
Repo: Comfy-Org/ComfyUI_frontend PR: 7617
File: src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue:301-308
Timestamp: 2025-12-18T16:03:02.066Z
Learning: In the ComfyUI frontend queue system, useQueuePendingTaskCountStore().count indicates the number of tasks in the queue, where count = 1 means a single active/running task and count > 1 means there are pending tasks in addition to the active task. Therefore, in src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue, enable the 'Clear Pending Tasks' button only when count > 1 to avoid clearing the currently running task. The active task should be canceled using the 'Cancel current run' button instead. This rule should be enforced via a conditional check on the queue count, with appropriate disabled/aria-disabled states for accessibility, and tests should verify behavior for count = 1 and count > 1.
```

471-480: Optimize containsSplatMesh to short-circuit traversal.

+   // Use a recursive helper that can short-circuit
+   function checkChildren(obj: THREE.Object3D): boolean {
+     for (const child of obj.children) {
+       if (child instanceof SplatMesh) return true
+       if (checkChildren(child)) return true
+     }
+     return false
+   }
+   return checkChildren(target)
  }
```

File: src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue:301-308
Timestamp: 2025-12-18T16:03:02.066Z
Learning: In the ComfyUI frontend queue system, useQueuePendingTaskCountStore().count indicates the number of tasks in the queue, where count = 1 means a single active/running task and count > 1 means there are pending tasks in addition to the active task. Therefore, in src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue, enable the 'Clear Pending Tasks' button only when count > 1 to avoid clearing the currently running task. The active task should be canceled using the 'Cancel current run' button instead. This rule should be enforced via a conditional check on the queue count, with appropriate disabled/aria-disabled states for accessibility, and tests should verify behavior for count = 1 and count > 1.
```

src/services/load3dService.ts (1)
78-102: Silent failure when splat model lacks originalURL.
The code correctly differentiates between splat models (which must be reloaded via URL) and cloneable models. However, when a splat model lacks an originalURL (line 79-82), the method silently skips model copying entirely, leaving the target without a model. This is a silent failure that could cause confusing behavior for users.
🔎 Recommended fix: Add error handling
```
 if (source.isSplatModel()) {
   const originalURL = source.modelManager.originalURL
   if (originalURL) {
     await target.loadModel(originalURL)
+  } else {
+    console.error('Cannot copy splat model: originalURL is missing')
+    throw new Error('Cannot copy splat model: originalURL is required for splat models')
   }
 } else {
```
This ensures the issue is surfaced to the developer/user rather than silently failing. If throwing is too aggressive for your use case, at minimum add the console.error to help with debugging.

     modelLoadingStart: () => {

     },
```

src/extensions/core/load3d/SceneModelManager.ts (1)
471-480: Consider short-circuiting the traversal for performance.
As noted in a prior review, traverse continues through the entire scene graph even after finding a SplatMesh. For large models, consider using a manual recursive traversal that can return early.
🔎 Optional optimization:
```
  containsSplatMesh(model?: THREE.Object3D | null): boolean {
    const target = model ?? this.currentModel
    if (!target) return false
    if (target instanceof SplatMesh) return true
-   let found = false
-   target.traverse((child) => {
-     if (child instanceof SplatMesh) found = true
-   })
-   return found
+   function checkChildren(obj: THREE.Object3D): boolean {
+     for (const child of obj.children) {
+       if (child instanceof SplatMesh) return true
+       if (checkChildren(child)) return true
+     }
+     return false
+   }
+   return checkChildren(target)
  }
```

+ private createSplatModel(url: string): THREE.Group {

  const fullUrl = path + filename

```

Timestamp: 2025-12-18T16:03:02.066Z
Learning: In the ComfyUI frontend queue system, useQueuePendingTaskCountStore().count indicates the number of tasks in the queue, where count = 1 means a single active/running task and count > 1 means there are pending tasks in addition to the active task. Therefore, in src/components/actionbar/ComfyActionbar.vue, enable the 'Clear Pending Tasks' button only when count > 1 to avoid clearing the currently running task. The active task should be canceled using the 'Cancel current run' button instead. This rule should be enforced via a conditional check on the queue count, with appropriate disabled/aria-disabled states for accessibility, and tests should verify behavior for count = 1 and count > 1.
```
